([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-assembly-handling))
- **reference-genome-base-conversion**: Use when building the nanoEM pipeline's first step: base-converted long reads (from EMseq/APOBEC treatment) must be mapped against a reference that mirrors their C-to-T and G-to-A substitutions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-base-conversion))
- **reference-genome-based-expected-abundance-computation**: Use when validating a long-read ARG profiler on sequenced mock communities: supplier reference genomes with known ARG content are available and a per-ARG expected abundance (cpg) profile is needed for precision/recall/Pearson evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-based-expected-abundance-computation))
- **reference-genome-binning-dataset-preparation**: Use when benchmarking a sequence-search pre-filter or distributed read mapper against reference collections that must be partitioned into bins, or when reproducing the Raptor Table 7 DREAM-Yara experiments on clustered NCBI RefSeq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/reference-genome-binning-dataset-preparation))
- **reference-genome-coverage-alignment**: Use when a merged or post-assembly contig set must be validated against reference genomes — e.g., checking that merging maintains reference coverage while reducing contig count and unmapped contigs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-coverage-alignment))
- **reference-genome-data-preparation**: Use when setting up ChiLin (or a bwa-based ChIP-seq QC pipeline) for a new species/build and the dependent data in db/ plus a mapped-ready genome index are not yet installed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/reference-genome-data-preparation))
- **reference-genome-database-preparation**: Use when a pipeline step requires a pre-built reference index (e.g., Bowtie 2 index of hs37d5 for host read removal) and the reference FASTA/index must be downloaded and prepared before annotation can start. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-database-preparation))
- **reference-genome-expanded-reference-construction**: Use when preparing a reference for alevin-fry/simpleaf single-cell RNA-seq quantification: you have a genome FASTA plus GTF/GFF3 annotation and need a spliced+intronic (splici) or spliced+unspliced (spliceu) expanded reference with a piscem index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/reference-genome-expanded-reference-construction))
- **reference-genome-fragmentation**: Use when comparing conspecific microbial strains across metagenomic assemblies or genomes with SynTracker and you need homologous genomic regions binned per sample from a single reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-fragmentation))
- **reference-genome-indexing**: Use when preparing an RNA-seq alignment reference: a genome FASTA plus Ensembl GTF must be compiled into a STAR index, or the reference must be re-built after masking known variant positions with Bedtools to reduce reference-allele bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/reference-genome-indexing))
- **reference-genome-indexing-and-mapping**: Decision support for choosing a FASTQ preprocessing tool: you have paired-end (PE150) FASTQ files from multiple filter tools plus a raw baseline, and need alignment-based metrics (mismatches, clips, single-read maps) against a reference genome to rank the filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/reference-genome-indexing-and-mapping))
- **reference-genome-masking-with-variants**: Use when RNA-seq-based ASE or allele-count analyses show inflated reference allele ratios (e.g., &gt;51%) and you need to quantify or mitigate mapping bias before calling ASE from biallelic SNP allele depths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/reference-genome-masking-with-variants))
- **reference-genome-preparation**: Use when starting an ONT SNV-calling benchmark or analysis and you need indexed reference assemblies (e.g. GRCh38, GRCm39, TAIR10, BDGP6) plus a miniSNV population-variant index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-preparation))
- **reference-genome-preparation-hla-augmented**: Use when preparing to align long-read (Oxford Nanopore or PacBio) whole-genome shotgun data for HLA typing with HLAminer, and a combined human+HLA-allele reference must be built and indexed before streaming minimap2 alignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-preparation-hla-augmented))
- **reference-genome-read-extraction**: Use when building a simulated long-read benchmark where ground-truth overlaps must come from aligning reads back to a known reference chromosome with defined accuracy, coverage, and read-length grid conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-read-extraction))
- **reference-genome-read-mapping**: Use when raw sequencing reads (e.g. RNA-seq FASTQ input) must be aligned to a reference genome as an early step of a Watchdog workflow before feature counting or differential expression testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/reference-genome-read-mapping))
- **reference-genome-selection**: Use when building a reduced, non-redundant species-level reference gene database (pan-genomes) from thousands of complete genomes to support gene prediction model selection on metagenomic contigs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-selection))
- **reference-genome-selection**: Use when choosing or recording the reference assembly and annotation for a non-human eukaryote project, or deciding on a new one. Not for liftover mechanics (reference-genomes-and-liftover) or building the assembly (genome-assembly-and-curation). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/reference-genome-selection))
- **reference-genome-sequence-extraction**: Use when you have a coordinate-indexed variant list (e.g. CHROM/POS from a GIAB/1KGp3 truth VCF) and need the surrounding reference bases from GRCh37 or GRCh38 to classify sequence context such as homopolymer runs (&gt;=4 identical bases) or STRs (&gt;=3 units of a 1-6 bp motif). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-sequence-extraction))
- **reference-genome-sequence-preparation**: Use when raw FASTQ reads must be aligned with BiSpark, or simulated bisulfite reads must be generated, and the reference genome (hg19 Build 37) has not yet been converted, indexed, or placed in HDFS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/reference-genome-sequence-preparation))
- **reference-genome-subsetting**: Use when a DNA simulation must target a specific chromosome and exome capture region, and subsetting workflow needs chromosome FASTA, padded BEDs, and region-restricted variant VCFs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/reference-genome-subsetting))
- **reference-genomes-and-liftover**: Use when choosing a reference build or contig naming, or converting coordinates and VCFs between builds. Not for general build bug checks (genomics-pitfalls) or alignment pipelines (sequencing-pipelines). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/reference-genomes-and-liftover))
- **reference-grade-product-film**: Direct and author premium short product films in Motionly or HyperFrames using carrier-first morphs, aggressive scale contrast, causal product proof, restrained Apple-like physicality, and semantic backgrounds. Use for launch ads, app promos, AI product films, or when generated motion feels static, generic, gradient-heavy, or disconnected. ([COPPSARY/Motify](https://github.com/COPPSARY/Motify/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/reference-grade-product-film) · ★89)
- **reference-guided-image-transformation**: Use for faithful reference-image transformations. ([zapabob/Skills](https://github.com/zapabob/Skills/tree/HEAD/archive/skill-md-reviewed/sha256/89/89bf879b27aa9de626868d9245f5e0bada19fa74e0adeb0d76d89e8f8a2de46b) · ★2)
- **reference-guided-read-alignment**: Use when paired-end Illumina RAD-seq reads (e.g., EcoRI-site reads from Populus parents) must be mapped to a reference genome to enable indel calling at restriction-site loci. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/reference-guided-read-alignment))
- **reference-guided-viral-genome-assembly**: Use when nanopore reads from an untargeted viral sequencing run have been trimmed, filtered, and de novo assembled, and a -scoring reference genome from a BLAST search in the virus reference database is available to guide consensus construction. I want that here" — the question is the trigger — or struggles to articulate a behavior they can recognize. - **reference-image-generate**: 基于当前仓库的可信文字设定和图像样本，从文字描述或草图生成新图片。用于按既定角色、服装、道具或视觉设计创作新构图，以及将草图完成为成图；已有成图的局部纠错使用同插件的 reference-image-repair。 ([Joycai/Joycai-agent-skill](https://github.com/Joycai/Joycai-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/plugins/reference-image-studio/skills/reference-image-generate))
- **reference-image-library**: Use when auditing or deduping a reference-image library. ([ponti-studios/kernel](https://github.com/ponti-studios/kernel/tree/HEAD/skills/reference-image-library) · ★1)
- **reference-image-repair**: 依据工作区可信参考修复已有图片，先分析差异并提出具体修改计划，等待用户确认或调整后再执行和核对。初始化及参考图片、文字设定的更新使用 reference-image-workspace。 ([Joycai/Joycai-agent-skill](https://github.com/Joycai/Joycai-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/plugins/reference-image-studio/skills/reference-image-repair))
- **reference-image-style-anchoring**: Uses one or more pre-generated style reference images to strongly anchor the visual look, lighting, and atmosphere of AI video generation, preventing the model from drifting to undesired aesthetics. - **reference-ingest**: 从 GitCode 等可按 API 枚举的**文档仓**批量摄取官方文档，沉淀为 reference 先验词条（面向训练/推理的中断、精度、性能定位）。编排「仓库文档面普查 → 增量扫描（path×blob sha 记账，防重复抓取与重复评估）→ 候选筛选 → 按需抓正文到本地缓存（ref-docs/，git-ignored，sha 校验）→ 抽取 → 走 to-reference 归类产出 → mark 回写判定」；状态文件 reference-ingest-state.json 是唯一台账，`skipped` 也记账（判过不沉淀的文档下一轮不再评估）。以 token 节省为设计约束：脚本只拉目录树、正文按候选抓、码块与日志裁剪后再读。这是「文档语料 → reference」的统一批量注入入口，与 issue-ingest（issue → case）、to-reference（零散来源 → reference）互补。 ([pillumina/ascend-sleuth](https://github.com/pillumina/ascend-sleuth/tree/HEAD/skills/reference-ingest) · ★6)
